ic/src/llm/CLAUDE.md:88:- **Session token** (default): `NEARAI_SESSION_TOKEN=sess_...`, base URL = `https://private.near.ai`. Tokens are persisted to `~/.ironclaw/session.json` (mode 0600) and optionally to the DB `settings` table (`nearai.session_token`). On 401 responses where the body contains "session" + "expired"/"invalid", `NearAiChatProvider` calls `session.handle_auth_failure()` which triggers the interactive OAuth login flow and retries once. Plain `AuthFailed` 401s are not retried.
ic/src/llm/CLAUDE.md:181:**Auth flow:** Device code OAuth via `auth.openai.com/api/accounts/deviceauth/*` endpoints. On first run, displays a code for the user to enter at a URL. Tokens are persisted to `~/.ironclaw/openai_codex_session.json` (mode 0600) and auto-refreshed before expiry.

Tokens are persisted to `~/.ironclaw/session.json` (mode 0600) and optionally to the DB `settings` table (`nearai.session_token`). Plain `AuthFailed` 401s are not retried.
